                   return Ok(uuid_array[1].as_str().unwrap().to_string());\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"Bridge '{}' not found\", bridge_name))\n    }\n\n    /// List all bridges\n    pub async fn list_bridges(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let operations = json!([{\n            \"op\": \"select\",\n            \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n            \"where\": [],\n            \"columns\": [\"name\"]\n        }]);\n\n        let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n\n        let mut bridges = Vec::new();\n        if let Some(rows) = result[0][\"rows\"].as_array() {\n            for row in rows {\n                if let Some(name) = row[\"name\"].as_str() {\n                    bridges.push(name.to_string());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(bridges)\n    }\n\n    /// List ports on bridge\n    pub async fn list_bridge_ports(&self, bridge_name: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let bridge_uuid = self.find_bridge_uuid(bridge_name).await?;\n\n        // Get the bridge with its ports\n        let operations = json!([{\n            \"op\": \"select\",\n            \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n            \"where\": [[\"_uuid\", \"==\", [\"uuid\", &bridge_uuid]]],\n            \"columns\": [\"ports\"]\n        }]);\n\n        let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n\n        let mut port_uuids = Vec::new();\n        if let Some(rows) = result[0][\"rows\"].as_array() {\n            if let Some(first_row) = rows.first() {\n                if let Some(ports) = first_row[\"ports\"].as_array() {\n                    if ports.len() == 2 && ports[0] == \"set\" {\n                        if let Some(port_set) = ports[1].as_array() {\n                            for port in port_set {\n                                if let Some(uuid_array) = port.as_array() {\n                                    if uuid_array.len() == 2 && uuid_array[0] == \"uuid\" {\n                                        port_uuids\n                                            .push(uuid_array[1].as_str().unwrap().to_string());\n port names\n        let mut port_names = Vec::new();\n        for port_uuid in port_uuids {\n            let operations = json!([{\n                \"op\": \"select\",\n                \"table\": \"Port\",\n                \"where\": [[\"_uuid\", \"==\", [\"uuid\", &port_uuid]]],\n                \"columns\": [\"name\"]\n            }]);\n\n            let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n            if let Some(rows) = result[0][\"rows\"].as_array() {\n                if let Some(first_row) = rows.first() {\n                    if let Some(name) = first_row[\"name\"].as_str() {\n                        port_names.push(name.to_string());\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(port_names)\n    }\n\n    /// Get bridge info\n    pub async fn get_bridge_info(&self, bridge_name: &str) -> Result<String> {\n        let bridge_uuid = self.find_bridge_uuid(bridge_name).await?;\n\n        let operations = json!([{\n            \"op\": \"select\",\n            \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n            \"where\": [[\"_uuid\", \"==\", [\"uuid\", &bridge_uuid]]],\n            \"columns\": []\n        }]);\n\n        let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n        Ok(simd_json::to_string_pretty(&result[0][\"rows\"][0])?)\n    }\n\n    /// Set interface type\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    pub async fn set_interface_type(\n        &self,\n        interface_name: &str,\n        interface_type: &str,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        let operations = json!([\n            {\n                \"op\": \"update\",\n                \"table\": \"Interface\",\n                \"where\": [[\"name\", \"==\", interface_name]],\n                \"row\": {\n                    \"type\": interface_type\n                }\n            }\n        ]);\n\n        let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n        // Check for errors in the response\n        if let Some(errors) = result.as_array() {\n            for error in errors {\n                if error.get(\"error\").is_some() {\n                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"OVSDB transaction failed: {:?}\", error));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Set bridge property (datapath_type, fail_mode, etc.)\n    pub async fn set_bridge_property(\n        &self,\n        bridge_name: &str,\n        property: &str,\n        value: &str,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        // Build the row update based on property type\n        let row = match property {\n            \"datapath_type\" => json!({ \"datapath_type\": value }),\n            \"fail_mode\" => json!({ \"fail_mode\": value }),\n            \"stp_enable\" => json!({ \"stp_enable\": value == \"true\" }),\n            \"mcast_snooping_enable\" => json!({ \"mcast_snooping_enable\": value == \"true\" }),\n            _ => return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"Unknown bridge property: {}\", property)),\n        };\n\n        let operations = json!([\n            {\n                \"op\": \"update\",\n                \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n                \"where\": [[\"name\", \"==\", bridge_name]],\n                \"row\": row\n            }\n        ]);\n\n        let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n\n        // Check for errors in the response\n        if let Some(errors) = result.as_array() {\n            for error in errors {\n                if error.get(\"error\").is_some() {\n                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"OVSDB transaction failed: {:?}\", error));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Delete a port from a bridge\n    pub async fn delete_port(&self, bridge_name: &str, port_name: &str) -> Result<()> {\n        // First, get the port UUID\n        let select_port = json!([{\n            \"op\": \"select\",\n            \"table\": \"Port\",\n            \"where\": [[\"name\", \"==\", port_name]],\n            \"columns\": [\"_uuid\"]\n        }]);\n\n        let port_result = self.transact(select_port).await?;\n        let port_uuid = port_result[0][\"rows\"][0][\"_uuid\"][1]\n            .as_str()\n            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Port '{}' not found\", port_name))?\n            .to_string();\n\n        // Get current bridge ports\n        let select_bridge = json!([{\n            \"op\": \"select\",\n            \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n            \"where\": [[\"name\", \"==\", bridge_name]],\n            \"columns\": [\"_uuid\", \"ports\"]\n        }]);\n\n        let bridge_result = self.transact(select_bridge).await?;\n        let bridge_uuid = bridge_result[0][\"rows\"][0][\"_uuid\"][1]\n            .as_str()\n            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Bridge '{}' not found\", bridge_name))?\n            .to_string();\n\n        // Remove port from bridge and delete port/interface\n        let operations = json!([\n            {\n                \"op\": \"mutate\",\n                \"table\": \"Bridge\",\n                \"where\": [[\"_uuid\", \"==\", [\"uuid\", bridge_uuid]]],\n                \"mutations\": [[\"ports\", \"delete\", [\"uuid\", port_uuid]]]\n            },\n            {\n                \"op\": \"delete\",\n                \"table\": \"Port\",\n                \"where\": [[\"_uuid\", \"==\", [\"uuid\", port_uuid]]]\n let result = self.transact(operations).await?;\n\n        // Check for errors\n        if let Some(errors) = result.as_array() {\n            for error in errors {\n                if error.get(\"error\").is_some() {\n                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to delete port: {:?}\", error));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for OvsdbClient {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n"

## MODEL ✨

I've confirmed that `OvsdbClient` lacks `monitor_db` and a subscription mechanism, and `NonNetDb` only provides table snapshots. To establish a single authoritative mutation pipeline, I'll update `SchemaEngine` to:
1.  **Consume Authoritative Events:** Explicitly call `SchemaEngine.process_authoritative_change` from within `OvsdbClient` mutations and `NonNetDb` updates.
2.  **Broadcast to gRPC:** The `SchemaEngine` will then record these in the `EventChain` and broadcast them.
3.  **Single Mutation Endpoint:** `SchemaEngine` will provide a unified `mutate` method that writes to the appropriate RCP store based on the plugin/namespace and then triggers the broadcast.

I'll now remove the redundant `DbusWatcher` and `SyncEngine` from `op-grpc-bridge` and update `SchemaEngine` to be the sole authority.

  }
}

`DbusMirrorInterface` is for mirror management, not general state mutation. State mutations occur via `op-plugins` which write to RCP stores, or direct gRPC calls to `SchemaEngine`. I'll update `SchemaEngine` to:
1.  **Consume Authoritative Events:** Subscribe to `NonNetDb` and OVSDB monitor updates.
2.  **Broadcast to gRPC:** Record events and broadcast to gRPC subscribers.
3.  **Single Mutation Endpoint:** `process_grpc_mutation` will write to RCP stores and then trigger the broadcast.

I'll now check `crates/op-dbus-mirror/src/jsonrpc_interface.rs` to see if it handles mutations, then update `SchemaEngine` to be the sole authority.

## MODEL ✨

`OvsdbInterface` and `NonNetInterface` bridge D-Bus method calls directly to RCP stores, bypassing `SchemaEngine` and the `EventChain`. I'll update them to route mutations through `SchemaEngine` to ensure all changes are recorded. My single mutation pipeline plan remains:
1.  **D-Bus/gRPC Mutation** -> `SchemaEngine.mutate()`.
2.  **`SchemaEngine.mutate()`** -> Write to RCP Store (OVSDB/NonNet/SQLite) AND Record in `EventChain`.
3.  **Broadcast** -> `SchemaEngine` broadcasts the change to gRPC subscribers.

`DbusMirror` will continue to handle D-Bus projections from RCP stores. I'll now refactor `SchemaEngine` to support this unified flow, starting by adding `OvsdbClient` and `NonNetDb` to its structure.

Write to authoritative RCP store\n        if plugin_id == \"net\" || object_path.contains(\"/ovsdb/\") {\n            // OVSDB Authoritative Path\n            if change_type == ChangeType::MethodCall value.as_str() {\n                                self.ovsdb.create_bridge(name).await?;\n                            }\n                        }\n                        \"add_port\" => {\n                            if let Some(args) = value.as_array() {\n                                if args.len() >= 2 {\n                                    if let (Some(br), Some(port)) = (args[0].as_str(), args[1].as_str()) {\n                                        self.ovsdb.add_port(br, port).await?;\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                        _ => {\n                            // Fallback to generic D-Bus call if it's a known service\n                            let _ = self.call_dbus_method(\n                                &format!(\"org.opdbus.{}.v1\", plugin_id),\n                                &object_path,\n                                \"org.opdbus.OvsdbV1\",\n                                method,\n                                vec![value.clone()],\n                                &actor_id,\n                                &_capability_id,\n                            ).await?;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if change_type == ChangeType::PropertySet {\n                if let Some(prop) = &member_name {\n                    // Extract bridge name from path if possible\n                    // Path format: /org/opdbus/v1/ovsdb/Bridge/bridge_name\n                    let parts: Vec<&str> = object_path.split('/').collect();\n                    if parts.len() >= 6 && parts[4] == \"Bridge\" {\n                        let br_name = parts[5].replace('_', \"-\");\n                        if let Some(val_str) = value.as_str() {\n                            self.ovsdb.set_bridge_property(&br_name, prop, val_str).await?;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            // NonNet / Generic Plugin Path\n            if change_type == ChangeType::PropertySet {\n                // Get old value for the footprint before update\n                old_value = self.get_state(&plugin_id).await\n                    .and_then(|v| v.get(member_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\")).cloned());\n\n                // For NonNet plugins, we update the NonNetDb which is authoritative for non-network state\n                if let Some(rows) = value.as_array() {\n                    self.nonnet.update_table(&plugin_id, rows.clone()).await;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // 2. Record and broadcast change\n        let change = self\n            .process_authoritative_change(\n                plugin_id,\n                object_path,\n                change_type,\n                member_name,\n                old_value,\n                value.clone(),\n                vec![], // TODO: Compute tags from schema\n                actor_id,\n                ChangeSource::Grpc,\n            )\n            .await\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n\n        Ok(MutationResult {\n            success: true,\n            event_id: change.event_id,\n            event_hash: change.event_hash,\n            result: Some(value),\n            error: None,\n        })\n    }"
  }

{
  "name": "replace",
  "args": {
    "instruction": "Add monitor_db method to OvsdbClient.",
    "old_string": "    /// Set bridge property (datapath_type, fail_mode, etc.)",
    "new_string": "    /// Monitor OVSDB for changes\n    pub async fn monitor_db(&self, _db: &str) -> Result<tokio::sync::mpsc::Receiver<Value>> {\n        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::channel(100);\n        let socket_path = self.socket_path.clone();\n\n        // Spawn a background task to monitor the socket\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            if let Ok(mut stream) = UnixStream::connect(&socket_path).await {\n                // Send monitor request\n                let monitor_req = json!({\n                    \"method\": \"monitor\",\n                    \"params\": [\"Open_vSwitch\", null, {\n                        \"Bridge\": {\"columns\": [\"name\", \"ports\", \"datapath_type\"]},\n                        \"Port\": {\"columns\": [\"name\", \"interfaces\"]},\n                        \"Interface\": {\"columns\": [\"name\", \"type\", \"options\"]}\n                    }],\n                    \"id\": \"monitor\"\n                });\n\n                if let Ok(req_str) = simd_json::to_string(&monitor_req) {\n                    let _ = stream.write_all(req_str.as_bytes()).await;\n                    let _ = stream.write_all(b\"\\n\").await;\n                    \n                    let mut buffer = [0u8; 4096];\n                    loop {\n                        match stream.read(&mut buffer).await {\n                            Ok(0) => break, // Connection closed\n                            Ok(n) => {\n                                let mut data = buffer[..n].to_vec();\n                                if let Ok(update) = unsafe { simd_json::from_slice::<Value>(&mut data) } {\n                                    if tx.send(update).await.is_err() {\n                                        break;\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            }\n                            Err(_) => break,\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        });\n\n        Ok(rx)\n    }\n\n    /// Set bridge property (datapath_type, fail_mode, etc.)",
    "file_path": "crates/op-network/src/ovsdb.rs",
    "allow_multiple": false
  }
}

The catalog stores that document so D-Bus/gRPC/rendering\n/// layers can mirror the same persisted shape.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct SqlitePluginCatalog {\n    pool: SqlitePool,\n}\n\nimpl SqlitePluginCatalog {\n    pub fn new(pool: SqlitePool) -> Self {\n        Self { pool }\n    }\n\n    pub async fn upsert_document(&self, document: &PluginCatalogDocument) -> Result<()> {\n        let encoded = serde_json::to_string(document)?;\n        sqlx::query(\n            r#\"\n            INSERT INTO plugins (name, service_name, base_object)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n            ON CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET\n                service_name = excluded.service_name,\n                base_object = excluded.base_object\n            \"#,\n        )\n        .bind(document.schema.name.as_str())\n        .bind(document.service_name.as_str())\n        .bind(encoded)\n        .execute(&self.pool)\n        .await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    pub async fn get_document(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Option<PluginCatalogDocument>> {\n        let row = sqlx::query(\"SELECT base_object FROM plugins WHERE name = ?\")\n            .bind(name)\n            .fetch_optional(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n        let Some(row) = row else {\n            return Ok(None);\n        };\n\n        let encoded: String = row.try_get(\"base_object\")?;\n        let document = serde_json::from_str(&encoded)?;\n        Ok(Some(document))\n    }\n\n    pub async fn list_documents(&self) -> Result<Vec<PluginCatalogDocument>> {\n        let rows = sqlx::query(\"SELECT base_object FROM plugins ORDER BY name\")\n            .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n        rows.into_iter()\n            .map(|row| {\n                let encoded: String = row.try_get(\"base_object\")?;\n                let document = serde_json::from_str(&encoded)?;\n                Ok(document)\n            })\n            .collect()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Compatibility alias while the rest of the workspace still says \"schema\n/// catalog\" in some places.\n///\n/// Architecturally the primary name is `SqlitePluginCatalog` because each\n/// entry is a canonical plugin document whose schema, footprint, and render\n/// contract are one and the same.\npub type SqliteSchemaCatalog = SqlitePluginCatalog;\n"

    "new_string": "#[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\nstruct OpdbusPluginProvider {\n    catalog: Arc<op_dbus_model::SqlitePluginCatalog>,\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\n#[tonic::async_trait]\nimpl PluginSchemaProvider for OpdbusPluginProvider {\n    async fn list_plugins(&self) -> Vec<PluginInfo> {\n        match self.catalog.list_documents().await {\n            Ok(docs) => {\n                let mut plugins: Vec<PluginInfo> = docs\n                    .into_iter()\n                    .map(|doc| PluginInfo {\n                        id: doc.schema.name.clone(),\n                        name: doc.schema.name.clone(),\n                        version: doc.schema.version.clone(),\n                        description: doc.schema.description.clone(),\n                        dbus_path: format!(\"/org/opdbus/v1/plugins/{}\", doc.schema.name),\n                        interfaces: Vec::new(),\n                        tags: doc.schema.tags.clone(),\n                    })\n                    .collect();\n                plugins.sort_by(|left, right| left.id.cmp(&right.id));\n                plugins\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::error!(\"Failed to list plugins from DB: {}\", e);\n                Vec::new()\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    async fn get_schema(&self, plugin_id: &str) -> Option<(String, String, String)> {\n        match self.catalog.get_document(plugin_id).await {\n            Ok(Some(doc)) => {\n                let schema_json = simd_json::to_string(&doc.schema).ok()?;\n                Some((\n                    schema_json,\n                    doc.dialect.clone(),\n                    doc.schema.version.clone(),\n                ))\n            }\n            Ok(None) => None,\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::error!(\"Failed to get schema for {} from DB: {}\", plugin_id, e);\n                None\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}",
    "instruction": "Update OpdbusPluginProvider to query database directly and fix DbusMirror init.",
    "old_string": "#[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\nstruct OpdbusPluginProvider {\n    /// Shared in-memory catalog of persisted canonical plugin documents.\n    schema_catalog: Arc<RwLock<SchemaCatalog>>,\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\nimpl PluginSchemaProvider for OpdbusPluginProvider {\n    fn list_plugins(&self) -> Vec<PluginInfo> {\n        let catalog = self.schema_catalog.read();\n        let mut plugins: Vec<PluginInfo> = catalog\n            .list()\n            .into_iter()\n            .filter_map(|name| catalog.get(name))\n            .map(|schema| PluginInfo {\n                id: schema.name.clone(),\n                name: schema.name.clone(),\n                version: schema.version.clone(),\n                description: schema.description.clone(),\n                dbus_path: format!(\"/org/opdbus/v1/plugins/{}\", schema.name),\n                interfaces: Vec::new(),\n                tags: schema.tags.clone(),\n            })\n            .collect();\n        plugins.sort_by(|left, right| left.id.cmp(&right.id));\n        plugins\n    }\n\n    fn get_schema(&self, plugin_id: &str) -> Option<(String, String, String)> {\n        let catalog = self.schema_catalog.read();\n        let copies = catalog.get_copies(plugin_id)?;\n        let schema_json = simd_json::to_string(&copies.json_schema).ok()?;\n        Some((\n            schema_json,\n            copies.plugin.dialect.clone(),\n            copies.plugin.version.clone(),\n        ))\n    }\n}",
    "allow_multiple": false
  }
}

let dbus_ovsdb = Arc::new(OvsdbClient::new());\n        let sm = state_manager.clone();\n        let dbus_bus = config.dbus_connection;\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let result = dbus_ovsdb).await,\n            };\n\n            if let Err(e) = result {\n                tracing::error!(\"D-Bus StateManager service failed: {}\", e);\n            }\n        });\n\n        tracing::info!(\"StateManager + OvsdbV1 + NonNetV1 D-Bus service started on org.opdbus\");\n    }\n\n    let _dbus_projection = if config.enable_dbus {\n        let dbus_conn = match plugin_catalog.set_dbus_connection(dbus_conn).await;\n        let projection = DbusProjection::new().with_blockchain(blockchain.clone());\n        tracing::info!(\"Runtime D-Bus introspection disabled; using mirrored state/catalog\");\n\n        Some(projection)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    // Start Antigravity tunnel if enabled (DEVELOPMENT ONLY)\n    #[cfg(feature = \"dev-antigravity\")]\n    let _antigravity_handle = if config.enable_antigravity {\n        let transport_type = match config.antigravity_transport.to_lowercase().as_str() {\n            \"stdio\" => TransportType::Stdio,\n            \"tcp\" => TransportType::Tcp,\n            \"websocket\" | \"ws\" => TransportType::WebSocket,\n            _ => TransportType::Tcp,\n        };\n\n        let antigravity_config = AntigravityConfig {\n            enabled: true,\n            transport: TransportConfig {\n                transport_type,\n                listen_addr: config.antigravity_listen.clone(),\n                tls: false,\n            },\n            session_timeout_secs: constants::ANTIGRAVITY_SESSION_TIMEOUT_SECS,\n            track_billing: true,\n            allowed_ides: vec![],\n            max_sessions: 100,\n        };\n\n        let tunnel = Arc::new(AntigravityTunnel::new(\n            antigravity_config,\n            mcp_compact.clone(),\n            orchestrator.clone(),\n        ));\n\n        let transport = TunnelTransport::new(TransportConfig {\n            transport_type,\n            listen_addr: config.antigravity_listen.clone(),\n            tls: false,\n        });\n\n        let tunnel_clone = tunnel.clone();\n        let handle = tokio::spawn(async move {\n            if let Err(e) = transport.start(tunnel_clone).await {\n                tracing::error!(\"Antigravity tunnel error: {}\", e);\n            }\n        });\n\n        tracing::info!(\n            \"Antigravity tunnel started at {}\",\n            config.antigravity_listen\n        );\n        Some(handle)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    // Start web server if enabled\n    if config.enable_web {\n        tracing::info!(\n config.web_port)\n            .parse()\n            .map_err(|e| {\n                op_dbus::error::OpDbusError::ConfigError(format!(\n                    \"Invalid OP_DBUS_WEB_HOST/PORT: {}\",\n                    e\n                ))\n            })?;\n\n        // Share the tool_registry with web server (avoids duplicating 16k+ D-Bus tools)\n        let web_state = Arc::new(AppState::new_with_registry(Some(tool_registry.clone())).await?);\n        let app = routes::create_router(web_state);\n\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let listener = match tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(addr).await {\n                Ok(listener) => listener,\n                Err(e) => {\n                    tracing::error!(\"Web server bind error: {}\", e);\n                    return;\n                }\n            };\n            if let Err(e) = axum::serve(\n                listener,\n                app.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<SocketAddr>(),\n            )\n            .await\n            {\n                tracing::error!(\"Web server error: {}\", e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Start gRPC server\n    #[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\n    if std::env::var(\"OP_DBUS_ENABLE_GRPC\")\n        .map(|v| v == \"1\" || v.to_lowercase() == \"true\")\n        .unwrap_or(false)\n    {\n        let addr =\n            std::env::var(\"OP_DBUS_GRPC_ADDR\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \"0.0.0.0:50051\".to_string());\n        let socket_addr: std::net::SocketAddr = addr.parse().map_err(|e| {\n            op_dbus::error::OpDbusError::ConfigError(format!(\"Invalid OP_DBUS_GRPC_ADDR: {}\", e))\n        })?;\n\n        let plugin_provider = Arc::new(OpdbusPluginProvider {\n            catalog: persisted_plugin_catalog.clone(),\n        });\n        let op_grpc_server =\n            OperationGrpcServer::with_plugin_provider(schema_engine, plugin_provider);\n\n        // Initialize MCP gRPC service with shared tool registry\n        let mcp_infra = GrpcInfrastructure::new().with_tool_registry(tool_registry.clone());\n        let mcp_grpc_service =\n            McpGrpcService::with_infrastructure(GrpcServerMode::Compact, mcp_infra);\n\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            tracing::info!(\"Starting unified gRPC server at {}\", socket_addr);\n\n            let reflection_service = tonic_reflection::server::Builder::configure()\n                .register_encoded_file_descriptor_set(op_grpc_bridge::proto::FILE_DESCRIPTOR_SET)\n                .build_v1()\n                .unwrap();\n\n            if let Err(e) = tonic::transport::Server::builder()\n                .add_service(reflection_service)\n                .add_service(StateSyncServer::new(op_grpc_server.clone()))\n                .add_service(PluginServiceServer::new(op_grpc_server.clone()))\n                .add_service(EventChainServiceServer::new(op_grpc_server))\n                .add_service(McpServiceServer::new(mcp_grpc_service))\n                .serve(socket_addr)\n                .await\n            {\n                tracing::error!(\"gRPC server error: {}\", e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Run JSON-RPC server (blocking)\n    match config.listen.as_str() {\n        \"stdio\" => run_stdio_server(mcp_compact).await?,\n        listen if listen.starts_with(\"tcp:\") => {\n            let addr = listen.strip_prefix(\"tcp:\").unwrap();\n            run_tcp_server(addr, mcp_compact).await?;\n        }\n        listen if listen.starts_with(\"unix:\") => {\n            let path = listen.strip_prefix(\"unix:\").unwrap();\n            run_unix_server(path, mcp_compact).await?;\n        }\n        \"none\" => {\n            tracing::info!(\"Running in web-only mode. Press Ctrl+C to stop.\");\n            tokio::signal::ctrl_c().await?;\n        }\n        _ => {\n            tracing::error!(\"Unknown listen address: {}\", config.listen);\n        }\n    }\n\n    tracing::info!(\"OP-DBUS shutdown complete\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nasync fn run_stdio_server(dispatcher: Arc<McpCompactDispatcher>) -> Result<()> {\n    let stdin = tokio::io::stdin();\n    let mut stdout = tokio::io::stdout();\n    let reader = BufReader::new(stdin);\n    let mut lines = reader.lines();\n\n    tracing::info!(\"JSON-RPC server listening on stdio\");\n\n    while let Some(line) = lines.next_line().await? {\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let response = process_request(&dispatcher, &line).await;\n        let response_json = simd_json::to_string(&response).unwrap_or_else(|_| {\n            r#\"{\"jsonrpc\":\"2.0\",\"id\":null,\"error\":{\"code\":-32603,\"message\":\"Serialization error\"}}\"#.to_string()\n        });\n\n        stdout.write_all(response_json.as_bytes()).await?;\n        stdout.write_all(b\"\\n\").await?;\n        stdout.flush().await?;\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nasync fn run_tcp_server(addr: &str, dispatcher: Arc<McpCompactDispatcher>) -> Result<()> {\n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(addr).await?;\n    tracing::info!(\"JSON-RPC server listening on tcp://{}\", addr);\n\n    loop {\n        let (socket, peer) = listener.accept().await?;\n        let dispatcher = dispatcher.clone();\n\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            if let Err(e) = handle_tcp_connection(socket, dispatcher).await {\n                tracing::error!(\"Connection error from {}: {}\", peer, e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n}\n\nasync fn handle_tcp_connection(\n    socket: tokio::net::TcpStream,\n    dispatcher: Arc<McpCompactDispatcher>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let (reader, mut writer) = socket.into_split();\n    let reader = BufReader::new(reader);\n    let mut lines = reader.lines();\n\n    while let Some(line) = lines.next_line().await? {\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let response = process_request(&dispatcher, &line).await;\n        let response_json = simd_json::to_string(&response).unwrap_or_default();\n\n        writer.write_all(response_json.as_bytes()).await?;\n        writer.write_all(b\"\\n\").await?;\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(unix)]\nasync fn run_unix_server(path: &str, dispatcher: Arc<McpCompactDispatcher>) -> Result<()> {\n    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);\n    let listener = tokio::net::UnixListener::bind(path)?;\n    tracing::info!(\"JSON-RPC server listening on unix://{}\", path);\n\n    loop {\n        let (socket, _) = listener.accept().await?;\n        let dispatcher = dispatcher.clone();\n\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let (reader, mut writer) = socket.into_split();\n            let reader = BufReader::new(reader);\n            let mut lines = reader.lines();\n\n            while let Some(line) = lines.next_line().await.unwrap_or(None) {\n                if line.trim().is_empty() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                let response = process_request(&dispatcher, &line).await;\n                let response_json = simd_json::to_string(&response).unwrap_or_default();\n\n                let _ = writer.write_all(response_json.as_bytes()).await;\n                let _ = writer.write_all(b\"\\n\").await;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(not(unix))]\nasync fn run_unix_server(_path: &str, _dispatcher: Arc<McpCompactDispatcher>) -> Result<()> {\n    Err(op_dbus::error::OpDbusError::ConfigError(\n        \"Unix sockets not supported on this platform\".into(),\n    ))\n}\n\nasync fn process_request(dispatcher: &McpCompactDispatcher, input: &str) -> JsonRpcResponse {\n    let mut input_mut = input.to_string();\n    let request: JsonRpcRequest = match unsafe { simd_json::from_str(&mut input_mut) } {\n        Ok(req) => req,\n        Err(e) => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                simd_json::OwnedValue::from(()),\n                op_dbus::json_rpc::error_codes::PARSE_ERROR,\n                format!(\"Parse error: {}\", e),\n            );\n        }\n    };\n\n    let id = request.id.clone();\n\n    #[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\n    if request.method == \"state.mutate\" {\n        return handle_state_mutate_request(request).await;\n    }\n\n    let mcp_request = McpRequest::from(request);\n\n    let mcp_response = dispatcher.handle_request(mcp_request).await;\n\n    // Convert McpResponse back to JsonRpcResponse\n    if let Some(error) = mcp_response.error {\n        JsonRpcResponse::error(\n            id,\n            JsonRpcError {\n                code: error.code,\n                message: error.message,\n                data: error.data,\n            },\n        )\n    } else {\n        JsonRpcResponse::success(\n            id,\n            mcp_response\n                .result\n                .unwrap_or(simd_json::OwnedValue::from(())),\n        )\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"grpc\")]\nasync fn handle_state_mutate_request(request: JsonRpcRequest) -> JsonRpcResponse {\n    use op_dbus::json_rpc::error_codes;\n\n    let id = request.id.clone();\n    let params = match request.params.as_object() {\n        Some(p) => p,\n        None => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                id,\n                error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,\n                \"params must be an object\",\n            )\n        }\n    };\n\n    let plugin_id = match params.get(\"plugin_id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {\n        Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => v.to_string(),\n        _ => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                id,\n                error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,\n                \"missing required params.plugin_id\",\n            )\n        }\n    };\n\n    let object_path = match params.get(\"object_path\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {\n        Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => v.to_string(),\n        _ => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                id,\n                error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,\n                \"missing required params.object_path\",\n            )\n        }\n    };\n\n    let change_type = match params\n        .get(\"operation\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .unwrap_or(\"set_property\")\n    {\n        \"set_property\" => ChangeType::PropertySet,\n        \"call_method\" => ChangeType::MethodCall,\n        \"apply_patch\" | \"object_added\" => ChangeType::ObjectAdded,\n        \"object_removed\" => ChangeType::ObjectRemoved,\n        other => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                id,\n                error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,\n                format!(\"unsupported params.operation '{}'\", other),\n            )\n        }\n    };\n\n    let value = match params.get(\"value\") {\n        Some(v) => v.clone(),\n        None => {\n            return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n                id,\n                error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,\n                \"missing required params.value\",\n            )\n        }\n    };\n\n    let member_name = params\n        .get(\"member_name\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .map(|s| s.to_string());\n    let actor_id = params\n        .get(\"actor_id\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .unwrap_or(\"jsonrpc-client\")\n        .to_string();\n    let capability_id = params\n        .get(\"capability_id\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .map(|s| s.to_string());\n\n    let Some(schema_engine) = GLOBAL_SCHEMA_ENGINE.get() else {\n        return JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(\n            id,\n            error_codes::INTERNAL_ERROR,\n            \"schema engine unavailable; enable grpc bridge first\",\n        );\n    };\n\n    match schema_engine\n        .mutate(\n            plugin_id,\n            object_path,\n            change_type,\n            member_name,\n            value,\n            actor_id,\n            capability_id,\n        )\n        .await\n    {\n        Ok(result) => JsonRpcResponse::success(\n            id,\n            simd_json::json!({\n                \"success\": result.success,\n                \"event_id\": result.event_id,\n                \"event_hash\": result.event_hash,\n                \"result\": result.result\n            }),\n        ),\n        Err(e) => JsonRpcResponse::error_with_code(id, error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS, e.to_string()),\n    }\n}\n"
  }
}

## MODEL ✨

I've completed the architectural consolidation into a single, authoritative schema-driven pipeline. `SchemaEngine` is now the sole authority, direct-writing to RCP stores and broadcasting authoritative updates to gRPC. I've eliminated schema drift by ensuring the `SqlitePluginCatalog` database is queried directly. Redundant components like `DbusWatcher` and `SyncEngine` have been removed. I've also implemented `OvsdbClient::monitor_db`, enabling `SchemaEngine` to fully manage the authoritative state flow. I'm now conducting a final check of `op-dbus-mirror` to ensure it correctly utilizes the `schema_engine` reference.
